# Skills · M · part 52

781 entries, maths-olympiad to matrix-announcement. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **maths-olympiad**: Un workflow multi-agents avancé conçu pour résoudre, vérifier et mettre en forme des preuves rigoureuses pour les compétitions de mathématiques (telles que l'IMO, Putnam, USAMO, AIME). Il s'appuie sur des agents de résolution parallèles, une vérification adversariale avec isolation de contexte, un "mode profond" (deep mode) pour le calcul borné, et une passe de présentation finale pour générer des preuves élégantes en LaTeX. L'objectif est d'optimiser la précision conditionnelle (ne proposer une solution que si elle est certaine) tout en interdisant formellement l'accès au web pour préserver… ([valorisa/Claude-Skills](https://github.com/valorisa/Claude-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/maths-olympiad) · ★20)
- **maths-render**: Automatically render ALL LaTeX math to Unicode plain text. ALWAYS use this skill — do NOT wait for an explicit trigger. If your response will contain ANY math at all (even a single x², α, integral, fraction, or equation), you MUST load this skill first and render via render_maths. Covers inline $...$, display $$...$$, \(\), \[\], \begin{...}, and raw LaTeX like \frac, \sum, \int, \sqrt, \alpha. Also covers explaining any math/science topic (physics, numerical analysis, linear algebra, etc.) where formulas naturally arise. ([drozd324/dotfiles](https://github.com/drozd324/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/opencode/.config/opencode/skills/maths-render))
- **maths-skill-finder**: Finds the right maths skill in the maths-engine library of over 1,300 skills and 2,500 question generators, by topic words, school year, Australian Curriculum (ACARA) code, domain or answer kind, and follows prerequisites to easier or harder skills. Use when someone names a topic, year level or curriculum code and you need a skill id before making questions, or when a learner is stuck and you need a skill to step back to. ([Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine](https://github.com/Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine/tree/HEAD/plugins/maths-engine-teacher/skills/maths-skill-finder) · 2 copies)
- **maths-tutoring**: Tutors a learner through maths practice one question at a time using the maths-engine tools. The engine generates the questions and marks every answer, so the agent never solves a question for the learner or marks by its own judgment. Use when a student wants to practise a maths topic, year level or curriculum code, wants questions to work through, or asks to be checked on an answer. ([Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine](https://github.com/Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine/tree/HEAD/plugins/maths-engine-tutor/skills/maths-tutoring))
- **maths-usage**: Picks Puck.Maths primitives and determinism rules: FixedQ4816, FixedVector3, rate accumulators, Pcg32XshRr, AliasTable, Fnv1aHash, rounding, saturation, primes. Law cases: maths-laws. ([ByteTerrace/Puck](https://github.com/ByteTerrace/Puck/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/maths-usage) · ★2)
- **maths-worksheets**: Builds printable maths worksheets, quizzes and tests with answer keys and several shuffled versions using the maths-engine tools. Turns a teacher's request (topic, year level, ACARA codes, number of questions, difficulty mix) into a paper spec, checks the questions by sampling, and writes HTML for students and a separate key. Use when a teacher asks for a worksheet, homework, quiz, test or exam paper, or for different versions to stop copying. ([Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine](https://github.com/Jondi-Studio-Public/maths-engine/tree/HEAD/plugins/maths-engine-teacher/skills/maths-worksheets))
- **mathscinet**: Fetch MathSciNet (MR) numbers for mathematical papers. Use when creating biblio notes and the msnumber field needs to be filled. Requires institutional MathSciNet access. - **mathsdock-diagrams**: Use whenever a teacher asks for a maths diagram, worksheet or other teaching resource and MathsDock tools (diagram_capabilities, create_diagram, create_worksheet, create_booklet) are connected. - **mathshow**: Render math as a boxed image inline in the Ghostty terminal. - **mathshow**: Use when writing mathematical or statistical notation in a response — estimator formulas, derivations, proofs, probability/expectation expressions, matrices, integrals, or any discussion of econometrics, statistics, or machine learning methods. Governs how to present math legibly in the terminal, where LaTeX does not render. ([groceryheist/mathshow](https://github.com/groceryheist/mathshow/tree/HEAD/skills/mathshow))
- **mathskills**: End-to-end mathematical modeling competition workflow covering problem interpretation, data auditing, model selection, implementation, validation, visualization, paper writing, and final review. Use when an AI assistant needs to solve or review a mathematical modeling problem, process competition attachments, produce reproducible results, or write a Chinese modeling paper. Designed for tool-capable assistants including Codex, Claude Code, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Copilot, and comparable agents. ([xiaofan-0625/Mathematical-Modeling-Skills](https://github.com/xiaofan-0625/Mathematical-Modeling-Skills/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★17)
- **mathstack**: Research-grade workflow for mathematical writing and proof checking. Routes natural mathematical requests into MathStack assistant mode, research framing, literature mapping, definitions, proof planning, proof audit, and paper drafting. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · 3 copies)
- **mathstack-assistant**: Use for natural-language mathematical research assistance in a MathStack-enabled project. Automatically applies claim tracking, proof auditing, method-fit checks, counterexample search, citation caution, computational-evidence status, and submission readiness without requiring the user to call specific workflows. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/mathstack-assistant) · 3 copies)
- **mathstack-cold-review**: Use for professor-facing Cold Review: an isolated critical check of a mathematical result, proof, draft, or AI answer that focuses on serious errors and blockers, separates minor remarks, and stops when the critical review saturates. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/mathstack-cold-review) · 2 copies)
- **mathstack-counterexample**: Attack mathematical claims by searching boundary cases, removed hypotheses, degenerate examples, and small models. Records counterexample attempts without treating failed search as proof. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/mathstack-counterexample) · 2 copies)
- **mathstack-definitions**: Extract, draft, and stabilize definitions, assumptions, notation, examples, and non-examples before proof work depends on them. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/skills/mathstack-definitions) · 2 copies)
- **mathstack-gap-triage**: Triage unresolved mathematical proof gaps, connect each GAP-* to blocked claims, rank severity, and propose resolution strategies without hiding or prematurely closing gaps. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/mathstack-gap-triage) · 2 copies)
- **mathstack-independent-critical-check**: Use for an isolated second-pass review of a mathematical result, proof, draft, or AI-produced answer. Finds critical correctness errors and submission blockers while deliberately separating them from minor remarks and avoiding endless low-value nitpicking. ([CyberSytener/MathStack](https://github.com/CyberSytener/MathStack/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/mathstack-independent-critical-check) · 2 copies)
- **mathstack-literature-map**: Build a citation-honest map of known mathematical results, adjacent work, possible blockers, and unverified sources. - **mathstack-method-fit**: Evaluate whether a proposed proof method actually fits a mathematical target claim. - **mathstack-paper-draft**: Draft mathematical paper sections from the claim ledger, literature map, notation, and proof plan while preserving claim statuses and visible gaps. - **mathstack-proof-audit**: Skeptically audit mathematical proofs for missing hypotheses, invalid implications, dependency cycles, citation misuse, status mismatches, and hidden proof gaps. - **mathstack-proof-plan**: Build a proof strategy and dependency graph for mathematical claims before drafting polished proof prose.
